# Project Coordinator, National Council for Mental Wellbeing

Version order used for "inflation" comparisons is tentative: mission-driven_v1, bullet-bank_v1 and tailored-policy-analyst_v1 (NCMW shown as Present) first, then the 2026-04 files, then the 2026-05 hie-ehr/tailored files, grants_v1 (2026-06), and the two master resumes (2026-08, 2026-09).

Scope notes
- 28 resume files carry an NCMW section (the archived byte-duplicate of condensed-ats_v1 is not cited separately).
- Several files have an export defect where bullets from other jobs sit under the NCMW heading (detail-oriented_v3, condensed-ats_v1, grants_v1, long-master-drafts_v2, mission-driven_v4, tailored-development-coordinator_v1, tailored-research-coordinator_v1). Those bullets were assigned to their true employer by content and are excluded here; only the NCMW bullets are used. hie-ehr_v1 and hie-ehr_v5 have bullets under the wrong other-employer headings (not under NCMW); their NCMW blocks are complete and used here.
- Placed elsewhere (fixed 2026-10-06): in tailored-development-coordinator_v1 the bullet "Managed volunteer and intern coordination across open-house and event programming, overseeing outreach logistics, scheduling, and on-site direction for 10-15 participants per event." sits under the NCMW heading but matches the Sanctuary Centers HMIS open-house work (10-15 volunteers and interns per event). It now sits in A-sanctuary-centers-housing-coordinator-20 (variants).
- Identical or near-identical files (long-master-drafts_v1 = _v2 for NCMW; master-resumes_v1 = _v2; mission-driven_v2 = _v3 = _v4) are counted as separate versions per the extraction brief, so a bullet found only in such a set is tiered consistent. The notes line says when that is the case.
- Publication (Plante, A., & Dixit, D.C. (2026, April 22). Chasing the High: Why Gambling Disorder Belongs in the Addiction Conversation. National Council for Mental Wellbeing. thenationalcouncil.org/gambling-disorder-and-addiction/) is recorded as an achievement block below and also appears in the PUBLICATIONS section of most resumes.
- Only the user decisions at Gate 1 are `verified`: NatCon26 "18+ sessions" and "6,000+" attendees, newsletter "8,000+" subscribers, and the May 2026 end date. Everything else is unverified.

## Title and date variants
- Title: 2026-10-06 user instruction: the NCMW role title is always "Project Coordinator" (C-N01 superseded). "Junior Project Coordinator" (TA7, TA8 and many older versions) and the division wordings below are variants only.
- TA4 and TA6: "Project Coordinator, Substance Use Continuum"; TA7 and TA8: "Junior Project Coordinator, Substance Use Continuum" (Oct 2025 - May 2026).
- "Junior Project Coordinator, Substance Use Continuum": mission-driven_v1, bullet-bank_v1, hie-ehr_v1, hie-ehr_v2, hie-ehr_v3, hie-ehr_v4, hie-ehr_v5, tailored-public-health-grants-ccbhc_v1, tailored-public-health-policy-research_v1, tailored-generalist-project-coordinator_v1, tailored-development-coordinator_v1 (conflict C-N01)
- "Junior Project Coordinator, Substance Use Strategy & Growth Continuum": mission-driven_v2, mission-driven_v3, mission-driven_v4, detail-oriented_v1, detail-oriented_v2, detail-oriented_v3, condensed-ats_v1, grants_v1, long-master-drafts_v1, long-master-drafts_v2, long-master-drafts_v3, tailored-executive-operations-support_v1, tailored-research-coordinator_v1, tailored-strategic-operations_v1 (conflict C-N01)
- "Junior Project Coordinator, Substance Use Continuum Growth & Strategy": tailored-policy-analyst_v1 (conflict C-N01)
- "Project Coordinator, Substance Use Continuum" (no "Junior"): master-resumes_v1, master-resumes_v2 (conflict C-N01)
- User decision at Gate 1 (2026-10-05): "add both written 'Job Title 1 | Job Title 2'." All four distinct titles are therefore written in the frontmatter `title:` joined with " | ".
- Employer location in every version: Remote, DC.

- Start: Oct 2025 in all 28 versions (no start-date conflict).
- End "Present": mission-driven_v1, bullet-bank_v1, tailored-policy-analyst_v1.
- End "Apr 2026": mission-driven_v2, _v3, _v4, detail-oriented_v1, _v2, _v3, condensed-ats_v1, grants_v1, long-master-drafts_v1, _v2, _v3, tailored-executive-operations-support_v1, tailored-research-coordinator_v1.
- End "April 2026": tailored-development-coordinator_v1, tailored-generalist-project-coordinator_v1.
- End "May 2026": hie-ehr_v1 to _v5, tailored-public-health-grants-ccbhc_v1, tailored-public-health-policy-research_v1, tailored-strategic-operations_v1, master-resumes_v1, master-resumes_v2.
- User decision at Gate 1 (2026-10-05): "NCMW end date is May 2026." Canonical end is 2026-05; Present and Apr 2026 stay as variants (conflict C-N02, resolved).
- Notion Job History (single-source context): department Substance Use Continuum; Remote, DC; organization described as the nation's leading behavioral health membership association with 3,200+ member organizations and 10M+ people served annually. Dates match the repo (Oct 2025 - May 2026).
- Title variant only (per the rule that the NCMW title is always Project Coordinator): "Project Coordinator, Substance Use Continuum" (_raw/notion/master-career-profile.md, section 2.1).
- Notion tailored resumes (2026-10-08): "Project Coordinator, Substance Use Continuum" (most of the ten), "Junior Project Coordinator, Substance Use Continuum" (one file) and "Program Coordinator, Substance Use Continuum" (one file). Division-name and seniority wording only; per the rule the title is always Project Coordinator. Dates Oct 2025 - May 2026 in all ten.

## Employer description (as written in resumes)
- (long-master-drafts_v1, long-master-drafts_v2) "The National Council for Mental Wellbeing (NCMW) is the nation's leading nonprofit membership association for mental health and substance use care, representing 3,200+ member organizations serving 10M+ people annually. This role sat within the Substance Use Continuum (SUC) program under the newly formed Growth & Strategy team, responsible for federal grant-funded program development, policy research, conference programming, e-learning product development, and cross-sector field-building."
- (detail-oriented_v3) "The National Council for Mental Wellbeing (NCMW) is the nation's leading nonprofit membership association for mental health and substance use care, representing 3,200+ member organizations serving 10M+ people annually. This role sat within the Substance Use Continuum (SUC) program under the newly formed Growth & Strategy team, responsible for federal grant-funded program development, policy research, conference programming, e-learning product development, and cross-sector field-building."
- (condensed-ats_v1) "Nation's leading nonprofit membership association for mental health and substance use care, representing 3,200+ member organizations serving 10M+ people annually. Role sat within a newly formed Growth & Strategy team responsible for federal grant-funded program development, policy research, conference programming, e-learning product development, and cross-sector field-building."
- (tailored-policy-analyst_v1, in-bullet form) "...within the newly formed Substance Use Continuum team inside the Strategy and Growth Office, the National Council's field-building unit responsible for SUD policy development, conference programming, educational product development, and cross-sector stakeholder engagement on behalf of 3,200+ member organizations serving more than 10 million people nationally."
- (master-resumes_v1, master-resumes_v2, in-bullet form) "...the nation's leading behavioral health membership association (3,200+ member organizations, 10M+ people served annually)..."
- Organizational-unit naming differs ("Growth & Strategy team", "Growth team", "Strategy and Growth Office"); see C-N15. [Resolved user 2026-10-06: names are longer/shortened/alternative names that are accurate.]

- (tailored-administrative-program-coordinator_v1) Built and maintained centralized internal systems for deliverable tracking across Asana, Excel, Airtable, CRM, grant portals, and shared drives, strengthening file management and reporting metrics while achieving 100% on-time deliverable completion despite shifting funder timelines

- (notion Responsibilities #12) Built and maintained centralized deliverable tracking systems across Asana, Excel, Airtable, HubSpot CRM, grant portal, and cloud storage
- (notion Achievements #15) On-time deliverable completion across 6+ workstreams: 100% on-time completion despite evolving federal funder timelines

- (mission-driven_v1) Coordinated and assembled end-to-end grant renewal responses across 6+ concurrent behavioral health workstreams, managing cross-functional input from clinical, policy, and program subject matter experts to meet submission requirements, internal quality standards, and stakeholder alignment.
- (condensed-ats_v1, tailored-executive-operations-support_v1) Coordinated end-to-end grant renewal responses across 6+ behavioral health workstreams, managing cross-functional input from clinical, policy, and program subject matter experts to meet federal submission requirements and quality standards.
- (long-master-drafts_v1, long-master-drafts_v2, long-master-drafts_v3) Coordinated and assembled end-to-end grant renewal responses across 6+ concurrent behavioral health workstreams, managing cross-functional input from clinical, policy, and program subject matter experts to meet federal submission requirements, internal quality standards, and stakeholder alignment across federal, internal, and member-facing deliverables.
- (hie-ehr_v5) Coordinated and contributed narrative content to grant applications and grant renewal proposals across 6+ concurrent behavioral health interventions, brainstorming work plan elements and proofreading for grammar and format accuracy to meet federal submission requirements.
- (master-resumes_v1, master-resumes_v2) Assembled end-to-end grant renewal and progress/final reports across 6+ workstreams, coordinating cross-functional input from clinical, policy, and program SMEs to meet federal submission requirements
- (tailored-cme-program-coordinator_v1) Assembled end-to-end grant renewal and progress/final reports across 6+ workstreams, coordinating cross-functional input from clinical, policy, and program subject matter experts to meet federal accreditation and reporting requirements
- (tailored-human-services-public-health-coordinator_v1) Assembled end-to-end grant renewal responses across multiple behavioral health workstreams, managing cross-functional input from clinical, policy, and program subject matter experts to meet federal submission requirements and quality standards.
- (notion federal-healthcare-project-coordination_v1) Assembled end-to-end grant renewal responses across 6+ concurrent workstreams, managing cross-functional input from clinical, policy, and program subject matter experts to meet federal submission requirements.
- (notion association-membership-programs_v1) Assembled progress and final reports across 6+ workstreams, coordinating input from clinical, policy, and program experts to meet funder deadlines.
- (notion executive-administrative-support_v1) Assembled end-to-end grant renewal, progress, and final reports across 6+ workstreams, coordinating cross-functional input from clinical, policy, and program subject matter experts to meet federal submission deadlines
notes: hie-ehr_v5 reframes as "coordinated and contributed narrative content to grant applications and grant renewal proposals ... brainstorming work plan elements and proofreading". The master resumes combine renewal and progress/final reports in one bullet (also listed under the progress-reports block).

- (hie-ehr_v5) Served as sole post-meeting communications owner for internal and external stakeholder meetings conducted via video conferencing (Zoom) and in-person sessions, preparing structured agendas, distributing same-day meeting notes, and tracking action items to completion across federal officials, clinicians, and executive leadership.

- (grants_v1) Served as sole post-meeting communications owner for all internal and external stakeholder meetings, producing structured agendas, distributing same-day meeting notes, and tracking action items to completion across federal officials, healthcare executives, clinicians, policymakers, and 15+ partner organizations.

variants:
- (mission-driven_v1) Coordinated cross-sector public health equity initiatives alongside SAMHSA, CDC, and other federal and provider partners, supporting grant-funded program delivery across health, behavioral health, and social services systems in alignment with integrative health, SDOH, and Health in All Policies (HiAP) frameworks.
- (bullet-bank_v1) Collaborated with internal teams and external stakeholders (including federal and provider partners) to support cross-sector initiatives and program delivery.
- (detail-oriented_v3, condensed-ats_v1, tailored-development-coordinator_v1, hie-ehr_v2) Coordinated cross-sector public health initiatives alongside SAMHSA, CDC, and federal partners, managing stakeholder scheduling, communications, and follow-through across concurrent deliverables.
- (long-master-drafts_v1, long-master-drafts_v2, long-master-drafts_v3, tailored-public-health-policy-research_v1) Coordinated cross-sector public health initiatives alongside SAMHSA, CDC, and other federal and provider partners in alignment with integrative health, social determinants of health, and Health in All Policies frameworks, managing stakeholder logistics and communications across concurrent deliverables.
- (tailored-executive-operations-support_v1) Coordinated multi-agency initiatives with SAMHSA, CDC, and federal, state, and local partners, managing scheduling, communications, and deliverable follow-through across concurrent workstreams while maintaining consistent responsiveness across all partner relationships.
- (hie-ehr_v1) Coordinated cross-sector public health initiatives alongside SAMHSA, CDC, and federal partners, managing stakeholder engagement, scheduling, and follow-through across concurrent deliverables while monitoring project risk and escalating issues to leadership as needed.
- (hie-ehr_v3) Coordinated cross-sector public health initiatives alongside SAMHSA, CDC, and federal partners and served as the point of contact for scheduling, communications, and followthrough across concurrent deliverables and shared responsibilities, applying structured tracking methods, problem solving, and adaptability flexible to evolving strategy, resource ideation, and deadlines.
- (hie-ehr_v4) Coordinated cross-sector public health initiatives alongside SAMHSA, CDC, and federal partners and served as the point of contact for scheduling, communications, and follow-through across concurrent deliverables and shared responsibilities, applying structured tracking methods, problem solving, and adaptability flexible to evolving strategy, resource ideation, and deadlines.
- (hie-ehr_v5) Coordinated cross-sector public health initiatives and technical assistance alongside SAMHSA, CDC, and federal partners, serving as the interpersonal point of contact for scheduling, communications, and follow-through across concurrent interventions and shared responsibilities, applying structured tracking methods, problem solving, and adaptability to evolving strategy, resource ideation, and deadlines.
- (tailored-public-health-grants-ccbhc_v1) Assisted development of cross-sector public health initiatives across healthcare executives, policymakers, federal agency partners (SAMHSA, CDC, CMS), internal and cross-functional teams as the initial point of contact, troubleshooting friction points, and facilitating timely and accurate information transfer, applying responsive interpersonal communication skills across audience and modalities..
- (tailored-strategic-operations_v1) Coordinated cross-sector public health initiatives alongside SAMHSA, CDC, and other federal and provider partners, managing stakeholder logistics and communications across concurrent deliverables in alignment with Health in All Policies and Integrative Health frameworks.
- (grants_v1) Coordinated cross-sector public health initiatives alongside SAMHSA, CDC, and federal partners, managing stakeholder communications, scheduling, and follow-through across concurrent federal agency, clinical, and partner organization relationships.
- (master-resumes_v1, master-resumes_v2) Coordinated cross-sector public health initiatives with SAMHSA, CDC, CMS, and federal partners, aligning deliverables with Health in All Policies, Integrative Health, and SDOH frameworks
- (tailored-cme-program-coordinator_v1) Aligned cross-sector public health initiatives with SAMHSA, CDC, CMS, and federal partners, ensuring program deliverables met Health in All Policies, Integrative Health, and SDOH framework requirements
- (tailored-human-services-public-health-coordinator_v1) Partnered with SAMHSA, CDC, and other federal agencies on cross-sector public health initiatives, managing stakeholder scheduling, communications, and follow-through across concurrent deliverables.
- (tailored-public-health-grants-ccbhc_v2) Served as primary point of contact for cross-sector public health initiatives, coordinating with healthcare executives, policymakers, and federal agency partners (SAMHSA, CDC, CMS) alongside internal and cross-functional teams, troubleshooting friction points and facilitating timely, accurate information transfer across audiences and modalities.
- (notion Responsibilities #26) Coordinated cross-sector public health initiatives with SAMHSA, CDC, and CMS, aligning to Health in All Policies and SDOH frameworks
- (notion federal-healthcare-project-coordination_v1) Liaised with SAMHSA, CDC, CMS, and other federal and provider partners on cross-sector public health initiatives, managing stakeholder logistics and communications across concurrent deliverables.
- (notion benefits-eligibility-program-coordination_v1) Partnered with cross-sector public health stakeholders including external partners (SAMHSA, CDC, CMS), subject matter experts (NCQA, NAATP), and member organizations (CCBHC, integrative health clinics), to align program delivery with Health in All Policies (HiAP), Integrative Health, and social determinants of health frameworks, managing stakeholder logistics and communications across concurrent deliverables.
- (notion public-health-multistakeholder-coordination_v1) Coordinated cross-sector public health policy initiatives with SAMHSA, CDC, and CMS, aligning program deliverables with Health in All Policies, Integrative Health, and social determinants of health frameworks.
- (notion client-service-project-support_v1) Coordinated cross-sector public health initiatives with SAMHSA, CDC, and CMS, aligning deliverables with Health in All Policies and Social Determinants of Health frameworks

variants:
- (mission-driven_v1) Coordinated end-to-end logistics for 18 national conference sessions at NatCon26 (National Council Annual Conference, Denver CO, April 2026) serving 6,000+ behavioral health sector attendees, managing presenter outreach and contracting, scheduling, prep calls, virtual platform setup, technical assistance, and post-event follow-up across 15+ organizations.
- (bullet-bank_v1) Coordinated logistics for 18 national conference sessions (6,000+ attendees), managing presenter outreach, scheduling, and prep calls across concurrent timelines.
- (tailored-policy-analyst_v1) Managed 18+ SUD-focused policy and clinical sessions at NatCon26 (largest national behavioral health conference; 6,000+ attendees), overseeing presenter outreach, marketing graphic and asset creation, collaboration and prep meetings, scheduling, platform setup, and post-session follow-up across concurrent timelines, utilizing Microsoft Office360 tools( Outlook, Teams, Powerpoint), with session topics spanning emerging policy and treatment landscape including GLP-1 receptor agonists and addictive behaviors, emerging efficacy and policy discussions involving Ibogaine and other psychedelics, value-based payment models for SUD outcomes, criminal-legal system-to-recovery pathways, and digital interventions in SUD care.
- (mission-driven_v2, mission-driven_v3, mission-driven_v4) Coordinated end-to-end logistics for 18 national conference sessions serving 6,000+ attendees, managing presenter outreach, scheduling, prep calls, virtual platform setup, technical assistance, and post-event follow-up across 15+ organizations including healthcare executives, clinicians, policymakers, and federal officials.
- (detail-oriented_v1) Coordinated end-to-end logistics for 18 national conference sessions serving 6,000+ attendees, managing presenter outreach, scheduling, prep calls, equipment setup, technical assistance, and post-event follow- up across 15+ organizations.
- (detail-oriented_v2) Coordinated end-to-end logistics for 18 national conference sessions serving 6,000+ attendees, managing presenter outreach, scheduling, prep calls, equipment setup, technical assistance, and post-event follow-up across 15+ organizations.

variants:
- (mission-driven_v1) Served as sole instructional designer, content author, and project manager for Recovery Ready, a 12-module foundational e-learning course covering substance use, SUD, overdose response, and recovery support, designed for universal access across personal, professional, and community settings.
- (tailored-policy-analyst_v1) Authored 12-module e-learning course on SUD, overdose response, and recovery support, developing all content from initial architecture through deployment and ensuring full compliance with applicable federal grant and accessibility standards including 2 CFR Part 200 and Section 508/WCAG.
- (detail-oriented_v3) Served as sole instructional designer, content author, and project manager for Recovery Ready, a 12-module foundational e-learning course covering substance use, SUD, overdose response, and recovery support — designed for universal access across personal, professional, and community settings.
- (condensed-ats_v1) Served as sole instructional designer, content author, and project manager for Recovery Ready, a 12-module e-learning course on substance use, SUD, overdose response, and recovery support; architected all modules in Articulate Rise 360, configured AI avatar narration via Synthesia, and administered the course on LearnWorlds LMS.
- (long-master-drafts_v1) Served as sole instructional designer, content author, and project manager for Recovery Ready, a 12module foundational e-learning course covering substance use, substance use disorder, overdose response, and recovery support, designed for universal access across personal, professional, and community settings.
- (long-master-drafts_v2, long-master-drafts_v3) Served as sole instructional designer, content author, and project manager for Recovery Ready, a 12-module foundational e-learning course covering substance use, substance use disorder, overdose response, and recovery support, designed for universal access across personal, professional, and community settings.
- (hie-ehr_v1) Served as sole instructional designer and project manager for a 12-module e-learning course on SUD and overdose response, overseeing the full lifecycle from content ideation and platform configuration to launch, while delivering operational support and federal compliance documentation.
- (hie-ehr_v2) Served as sole instructional designer and project manager for a 12-module e-learning course on SUD and overdose response, managing full lifecycle, including content ideation, design, platform configuration, and federal compliance documentation.
- (hie-ehr_v3, hie-ehr_v4) Served as sole instructional designer and project manager for a 12-module e-learning course on SUD and overdose response, overseeing full implementation from content ideation and design to platform configuration and federal compliance documentation.
- (hie-ehr_v5) Served as sole instructional designer and project manager for a 12-module e-learning educational program on SUD and overdose response, owning the full lifecycle from curriculum planning and educational materials development through LearnWorlds LMS configuration, learner enrollment, and participation tracking, plus federal compliance documentation.
- (tailored-public-health-grants-ccbhc_v1) Served as sole instructional designer and project manager for a 12-module e-learning course on substance use, SUD, and recovery support spanning full project lifecycle, adopting innovative AI tools and platforms to enhance and optimize production and delivery, and piloting survey tools (Jotform) in order to evaluate student feedback for ongoing adjustment and continuous improvement.
- (tailored-strategic-operations_v1) Served as sole instructional designer, supporting content author, and project manager for 12-module e-learning course on substance use, SUD, overdose response, and recovery support, managing the full development lifecycle from architecture through scripting, narration, and LMS deployment.
- (grants_v1) Developed Recovery Ready, a 12-module foundational e-learning course grounding behavioral health workforce education in evidence from clinical research, SAMHSA frameworks, and peer-reviewed literature on SUD, overdose response, treatment pathways, and the social determinants of health.
- (master-resumes_v1, master-resumes_v2) Served as sole instructional designer, content author, and project manager for Recovery Ready, a 12-module e-learning course on SUD and recovery support, owning the full lifecycle from architecture through LMS deployment
- (tailored-administrative-program-coordinator_v1) [combined bullet, also covers A-ncmw-project-coordinator-58] Authored a 12-module e-learning course from architecture through LMS deployment, producing accessible visual assets, editing instructional video content, and supporting Section 508/WCAG 2.1 standards before synthesizing learner feedback into leadership recommendations
- (tailored-cme-program-coordinator_v1) Owned full lifecycle instructional design and project management for Recovery Ready, a 12-module national e-learning course on SUD and recovery support, from architecture through LMS deployment
- (tailored-human-services-public-health-coordinator_v1) Authored and project-managed Recovery Ready, 12-module e-learning course on substance use, SUD, overdose response, and recovery support, serving as sole instructional designer for content used across professional, community, and peer-learning settings.
- (tailored-public-health-grants-ccbhc_v2) Served as sole instructional designer and project manager for a 12-module e-learning course on substance use, SUD, and recovery support, piloting Jotform surveys to evaluate learner feedback and inform ongoing improvement.
- (notion benefits-eligibility-program-coordination_v1) Led end-to-end instructional design and program management for Recovery Ready, a 12-module national e-learning initiative on substance use and recovery support, overseeing the full lifecycle from architecture through content development, accessibility review, and LMS deployment, ensuring all course materials met Section 508 and WCAG 2.1 federal accessibility standards.
- (notion professional-education-programs_v1) Developed a 12-module e-learning course from concept through LMS deployment, serving as sole instructional designer and content author for a national training initiative.
notes: Ownership wording differs (sole / "supporting content author" / co-developed / "developed" / "authored"). Several versions drop the "Recovery Ready" name. See C-N06. ### A-ncmw-project-coordinator-58
canonical: Architected and authored all 12 modules in Articulate Rise 360, covering: Orientation and Mindset; Foundations of Substance Use and SUD (DSM-5 criteria, brain physiology, dependence vs. problematic use); Signs, Symptoms, and Stages of Change; Language, Stigma, and Bias; Substance Use Isn't One Thing; Prevention and Continuum Capital; The Trajectory of Recovery; Crisis Readiness and Overdose Response; Treatment and Recovery Pathways (CBT, DBT, ACT, MI, CM, MAT); Non-Clinical Pathways; Other Pathways (natural recovery, digital therapeutics, telehealth); Course Conclusion.
